                        elseif item:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or item:IsA("Trail") then
                            item.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
                        elseif item:IsA("Sparkles") or item:IsA("Smoke") or item:IsA("Fire")
                            or item:IsA("PointLight") or item:IsA("SpotLight") or item:IsA("SurfaceLight")
                            or item:IsA("Beam") or item:IsA("Highlight") then
                            item.Enabled = false
                        end
                        if os.clock() - sliceStarted >= 0.0042 then task.wait(); sliceStarted = os.clock() end
                    end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local optimizer = LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("OptimizerClientActor")
                if optimizer and optimizer.SendMessage then optimizer:SendMessage("Optimize", true) end
            end)
            InstallFPSBoostHooks()
        end)
    end
    local DracoAuraHooked = false
    local DracoAuraQueue, DracoAuraQueued = {}, {}
    local DracoAuraProcessing = false
    local DracoAuraScanning = false
    local DracoHidden = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local DracoAuraBodyParts = {
        Head = true,
        Torso = true,
        HumanoidRootPart = true,
        UpperTorso = true,
        LowerTorso = true,
        LeftUpperArm = true,
        LeftLowerArm = true,
        LeftHand = true,
        RightUpperArm = true,
        RightLowerArm = true,
        RightHand = true,
        LeftUpperLeg = true,
        LeftLowerLeg = true,
        LeftFoot = true,
        RightUpperLeg = true,
        RightLowerLeg = true,
        RightFoot = true,
    }
    local DracoAuraKeywords = {
        "aura", "domain", "field", "sphere", "dome", "orb",
        "vfx", "effect", "spark", "symbol", "ring", "circle",
        "ember", "flame", "fire", "smoke", "glow", "particle",
        "trail", "beam", "highlight", "light", "flash",
    }
    local function DracoNormalizeName(name)
        return tostring(name):lower():gsub("[%s_%-%./:]", "")
    end
    local function IsDracoName(name)
        return DracoNormalizeName(name):find("draco", 1, true) ~= nil
    end
    local function HasDracoKeyword(name)
        local n = DracoNormalizeName(name)
        for _, key in ipairs(DracoAuraKeywords) do
            if n:find(key, 1, true) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function IsHornWingTail(name)
        local n = DracoNormalizeName(name)
        return n:find("horn", 1, true) ~= nil
            or n:find("wing", 1, true) ~= nil
            or n:find("tail", 1, true) ~= nil
    end
    local function InHornWingTail(obj)
        local node = obj
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            if not node then return false end
            if IsHornWingTail(node.Name) then return true end
            node = node.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function HasDracoAncestor(obj)
        local node = obj
        for _ = 1, 16 do
            if not node then return false end
            if node:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(node) then
                return false
            end
            if IsDracoName(node.Name) then
                return true
            end
            node = node.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function FindDracoCharacter(obj)
        local node = obj
        for _ = 1, 16 do
            if not node then return nil end
            if node:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(node) then
                return node
            end
            node = node.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end
    local DracoMarkerNames = {
        DracoV4 = true, DracoHornsB = true, DracoTail = true,
        DracoV4Aura = true, DracoAura = true,
    }
    local function HasDracoV4Marker(char)
        if not char then return false end
        -- One traversal instead of five recursive FindFirstChild calls.
        for _, desc in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if DracoMarkerNames[desc.Name] then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function IsOrangeAuraPart(obj)
        if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        if DracoAuraBodyParts[obj.Name] or InHornWingTail(obj) then return false end
        local size = obj.Size
        if math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) < 12 then return false end
        if obj.Transparency >= 0.98 then return false end
        local color = obj.Color
        return color.R >= 0.55 and color.G >= 0.15 and color.G <= 0.8 and color.B <= 0.45
    end
    local function IsLooseAuraPart(obj)
        if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        if DracoAuraBodyParts[obj.Name] or InHornWingTail(obj) then return false end
        local size = obj.Size
        if math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) < 12 then return false end
        return obj.Transparency < 0.98 and (obj.Transparency >= 0.2 or obj.Material == Enum.Material.Neon)
    end
    local function IsAuraLikePart(obj)
        return IsOrangeAuraPart(obj) or IsLooseAuraPart(obj)
    end
    local function IsInsideAuraObject(obj, allowLoose)
        local node = obj and obj.Parent
        for _ = 1, 8 do
            if not node then return false end
            if IsOrangeAuraPart(node) or (allowLoose and IsLooseAuraPart(node)) then
                return true
            end
            node = node.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function HasActiveDracoAura(char, markerKnown)
        if not char then return false end
        if not markerKnown and not HasDracoV4Marker(char) then return false end
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if IsAuraLikePart(obj) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    -- Cache per-character Draco state briefly so processing hundreds of queued
    -- parts does not re-scan the whole character every time.
    local DracoCharState = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function GetDracoCharState(char)
        local now = os.clock()
        local cached = DracoCharState[char]
        if cached and now - cached.at < 1 then
            return cached.marker, cached.active
        end
        local marker = HasDracoV4Marker(char)
        local active = marker and HasActiveDracoAura(char, true) or false
        DracoCharState[char] = { at = now, marker = marker, active = active }
        return marker, active
    end
    local function IsAuraVisualEffect(obj)
        return obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Sparkles")
            or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Trail")
            or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke")
            or obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight")
            or obj:IsA("Highlight")
    end
    local function ShouldHideDracoObject(obj, aggressive)
        if not obj or not obj.Parent then return false end
        local dracoRelated = HasDracoAncestor(obj)
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            if DracoAuraBodyParts[obj.Name] or InHornWingTail(obj) then return false end
            if dracoRelated then return true end
            if aggressive and HasDracoKeyword(obj.Name) then return true end
            if aggressive and IsAuraLikePart(obj) then return true end
            return false
        end
        if IsAuraVisualEffect(obj) then
            return dracoRelated or IsInsideAuraObject(obj, aggressive)
                or (aggressive and HasDracoKeyword(obj.Name))
        end
        if obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            return dracoRelated or IsInsideAuraObject(obj, aggressive)
                or (aggressive and HasDracoKeyword(obj.Name))
        end
        if obj:IsA("BillboardGui") or obj:IsA("SurfaceGui") or obj:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            return dracoRelated or IsInsideAuraObject(obj, aggressive)
                or (aggressive and HasDracoKeyword(obj.Name))
        end
        return false
    end
    local function HideDracoVisual(obj)
        if not obj then return end
        DracoHidden[obj] = true
        pcall(function()
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                -- Guarded writes: this runs again every 2 s over every hidden
                -- object, so skip properties that are already set.
                if obj.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= 1 then obj.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
                if obj.Transparency ~= 1 then obj.Transparency = 1 end
                if obj.CanCollide then obj.CanCollide = false end
                if obj.CanTouch then obj.CanTouch = false end
                if obj.CanQuery then obj.CanQuery = false end
                if obj.CastShadow then obj.CastShadow = false end
            elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                if obj.Enabled then obj.Enabled = false end
                if obj.Rate ~= 0 then obj.Rate = 0 end
                local lifetime = obj.Lifetime
                if lifetime.Min ~= 0 or lifetime.Max ~= 0 then
                    obj.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
                end
            elseif obj:IsA("Sparkles") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke")
                or obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight")
                or obj:IsA("Highlight") then
                if obj.Enabled then obj.Enabled = false end
            elseif obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Trail") then
                if obj.Enabled then obj.Enabled = false end
                if obj:IsA("Trail") then
                    local lifetime = obj.Lifetime
                    if lifetime.Min ~= 0 or lifetime.Max ~= 0 then
                        obj.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
                    end
                elseif obj.Lifetime ~= 0 then
                    obj.Lifetime = 0
                end
            elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
                if obj.Transparency ~= 1 then obj.Transparency = 1 end
            elseif obj:IsA("BillboardGui") or obj:IsA("SurfaceGui") or obj:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                if obj.Enabled then obj.Enabled = false end
            end
        end)
    end
    local function HideDracoTree(root, aggressive)
        if not root or not _G.RemoveDracoAura then return end
        if ShouldHideDracoObject(root, aggressive) then
            HideDracoVisual(root)
        end
        local canHaveChildren = root:IsA("Model") or root:IsA("Folder")
            or root:IsA("BasePart") or root:IsA("Attachment")
        if not canHaveChildren then return end
        for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            if not _G.RemoveDracoAura then return end
            if ShouldHideDracoObject(obj, aggressive) then
                HideDracoVisual(obj)
            end
        end
    end
    local function CleanDracoRoot(root, aggressive)
        HideDracoTree(root, aggressive)
    end
    local function CleanDracoCharacter(char, aggressive)
        HideDracoTree(char, aggressive)
    end
    local function NearDracoV4(position, ctx)
        if ctx then
            local positions = ctx.positions
            for i = 1, #positions do
                if (positions[i] - position).Magnitude <= 120 then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local char = player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp and HasDracoV4Marker(char) then
                if (hrp.Position - position).Magnitude <= 120 then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    -- Build the Draco marker positions once per scan instead of re-checking every
    -- player for every workspace part.
    local function BuildDracoScanContext()
        local positions = {}
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local char = player.Character
            if char and HasDracoV4Marker(char) then
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then positions[#positions + 1] = hrp.Position end
            end
        end
        return { positions = positions }
    end
    local function IsSeparateAuraPart(obj, ctx)
        if not IsLooseAuraPart(obj) then return false end
        if obj.Transparency < 0.2 then return false end
        if FindDracoCharacter(obj) or IsFPSBoostMapObject(obj) then return false end
        return NearDracoV4(obj.Position, ctx)
    end
    local function CleanWorkspaceDracoNames()
        if not _G.RemoveDracoAura then return end
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if not _G.RemoveDracoAura then return end
            if IsDracoName(obj.Name) then
                local isPlayerCharacter = obj:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj) ~= nil
                if not isPlayerCharacter and not IsFPSBoostMapObject(obj) then
                    pcall(CleanDracoRoot, obj, true)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function NearestPartOf(obj)
        local node = obj
        for _ = 1, 8 do
            if not node then return nil end
            if node:IsA("BasePart") then return node end
            node = node.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function ProcessDracoAuraQueue()
        if DracoAuraProcessing then return end
        DracoAuraProcessing = true
        task.spawn(function()
            while #DracoAuraQueue > 0 do
                local started = os.clock()
                local total = #DracoAuraQueue
                local head = 1
                local ctx = nil
                while head <= total do
                    local item = DracoAuraQueue[head]
                    DracoAuraQueue[head] = nil
                    head = head + 1
                    DracoAuraQueued[item] = nil
                    if _G.RemoveDracoAura and item.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            if IsDracoName(item.Name) then
                                HideDracoTree(item, true)
                                return
                            end
                            if HasDracoAncestor(item) then
                                HideDracoTree(item, true)
                                return
                            end
                            local char = FindDracoCharacter(item)
                            if char then
                                local marker, active = GetDracoCharState(char)
                                if marker then
                                    HideDracoTree(item, active)
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                            local part = NearestPartOf(item)
                            if part then
                                ctx = ctx or BuildDracoScanContext()
                                if #ctx.positions > 0 and NearDracoV4(part.Position, ctx)
                                    and (IsOrangeAuraPart(part) or IsSeparateAuraPart(part, ctx)) then
                                    HideDracoTree(part, true)
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    if os.clock() - started >= 0.004 then break end
                end
                if head > total then
                    DracoAuraQueue = {}
                else
                    local rest = table.create(total - head + 1)
                    local r = 0
                    for i = head, total do
                        r = r + 1
                        rest[r] = DracoAuraQueue[i]
                    end
                    DracoAuraQueue = rest
                end
                task.wait()
            end
            DracoAuraProcessing = false
        end)
    end
    local DracoActiveNow
    local function QueueDracoAura(obj)
        if not _G.RemoveDracoAura or not obj or DracoAuraQueued[obj] then return end
        local dracoName = IsDracoName(obj.Name)
        if not dracoName then
            -- Fast bail while nobody has Draco equipped: avoids touching every
            -- object the map streams in.
            if not (DracoActiveNow and DracoActiveNow()) then return end
            local visual = obj:IsA("BasePart") or IsAuraVisualEffect(obj)
                or obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture")
                or obj:IsA("BillboardGui") or obj:IsA("SurfaceGui")
                or obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("Folder") or obj:IsA("Attachment")
            if not visual then return end
            if IsFPSBoostMapObject(obj) then return end
        end
        DracoAuraQueued[obj] = true
        DracoAuraQueue[#DracoAuraQueue + 1] = obj
        ProcessDracoAuraQueue()
    end
    local function HasAnyDracoMarker()
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if HasDracoV4Marker(player.Character) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local DracoPresenceAt, DracoPresenceValue = 0, false
    DracoActiveNow = function()
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - DracoPresenceAt < 1 then return DracoPresenceValue end
        DracoPresenceAt = now
        DracoPresenceValue = HasAnyDracoMarker()
        return DracoPresenceValue
    end
    local function ScanWorkspaceDraco()
        if DracoAuraScanning or not _G.RemoveDracoAura then return end
        local ctx = BuildDracoScanContext()
        if #ctx.positions == 0 then return end
        DracoAuraScanning = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local sliceStarted = os.clock()
            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if not _G.RemoveDracoAura then break end
                -- Only draco-named objects or base parts can be hidden here;
                -- everything else is skipped without any ancestry checks.
                local dracoName = IsDracoName(obj.Name)
                if dracoName or obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if IsFPSBoostMapObject(obj) then return end
                        if dracoName then
                            local isPlayerCharacter = obj:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj) ~= nil
                            if not isPlayerCharacter then
                                HideDracoTree(obj, true)
                            end
                        elseif NearDracoV4(obj.Position, ctx)
                            and (IsOrangeAuraPart(obj) or IsSeparateAuraPart(obj, ctx)) then
                            HideDracoTree(obj, true)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if os.clock() - sliceStarted >= 0.004 then
                    task.wait()
                    sliceStarted = os.clock()
                end
            end
            DracoAuraScanning = false
        end)
    end
    local function InstallDracoAuraHooks()
        if DracoAuraHooked then return end
        DracoAuraHooked = true
        pcall(function() workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(QueueDracoAura) end)
        task.spawn(function()
            local wasPresent = false
            while true do
                task.wait(2)
                if _G.RemoveDracoAura then
                    local present = false
                    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        local char = player.Character
                        if char then
                            local marker, active = GetDracoCharState(char)
                            if marker then present = true end
                            if active then
                                pcall(CleanDracoCharacter, char, true)
                            elseif marker then
                                pcall(CleanDracoCharacter, char, false)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    -- When Draco first appears, sweep whatever the map already
                    -- streamed in before the marker existed.
                    if present and not wasPresent then
                        pcall(ScanWorkspaceDraco)
                    end
                    wasPresent = present
                    pcall(CleanWorkspaceDracoNames)
                    for obj in pairs(DracoHidden) do
                        if not obj.Parent then
                            DracoHidden[obj] = nil
                        else
                            pcall(HideDracoVisual, obj)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    PerformanceAPI.ApplyDracoAuraRemoval = function()
        InstallDracoAuraHooks()
        if not _G.RemoveDracoAura then return end
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local char = player.Character
            if char then
                if HasActiveDracoAura(char) then
                    pcall(CleanDracoCharacter, char, true)
                elseif HasDracoV4Marker(char) then
                    pcall(CleanDracoCharacter, char, false)
                end
            end
        end
        pcall(CleanWorkspaceDracoNames)
        ScanWorkspaceDraco()
    end
end
local SailNoclipOn = false
local function ApplySailNoclip(on)
    local boats = workspace:FindFirstChild("Boats")
    if boats then
        for _, boat in ipairs(boats:GetChildren()) do
            local target = not on
            for _, d in ipairs(boat:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide ~= target then
                    d.CanCollide = target
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local char = p.Character
        if char then
            for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local target
                    if on then
                        target = false
                    else
                        target = d.Name == "HumanoidRootPart"
                            or d.Name:find("LowerTorso") ~= nil
                            or d.Name:find("UpperTorso") ~= nil
                    end
                    if d.CanCollide ~= target then
                        d.CanCollide = target
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
RestoreWallClip = function()
    pcall(ApplySailNoclip, false)
end
_G.Abyss_ResetMapNoclipState = function()
    if not _G.AutoLeviathanFull then EnchantHandoff.Release() end
    RestoreWallClip()
end
task.spawn(function()
    local lastApplied, lastApplyAt = nil, 0
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        local on = (os.clock() - LastSailAt) < 0.5
        if on or SailNoclipOn then
            local now = os.clock()
            -- Re-apply only on state change or every 0.5 s while sailing.
            if on ~= lastApplied or now - lastApplyAt >= 0.5 then
                pcall(ApplySailNoclip, on)
                lastApplied, lastApplyAt = on, now
            end
        else
            lastApplied = nil
        end
        SailNoclipOn = on
    end
end)
do
local Abyss_AimPos, Abyss_AimTargetPart = nil, nil
local Abyss_AimOn, Abyss_AimLastAt = false, 0
local Abyss_AimToken, Abyss_AimRewrites = 0, 0
local Abyss_AimHookReady, Abyss_AimHookFailed, Abyss_AimHookMT, Abyss_AimHookOld = false, false, nil, nil
local Abyss_MouseHookReady, Abyss_MouseMT, Abyss_MouseStore = false, nil, nil
local Abyss_MouseOldIndex, Abyss_MouseOldNewIndex, Abyss_MouseMod = nil, nil, nil
local Abyss_InstallAimHook, Abyss_InstallMouseHook
local function Abyss_AimGate()
    return _G.AutoLeviathanFull and _G.SilentAim and not NavigationOwner
end
local function Abyss_RootOf(model)
    if not model then
        return nil
    end
    return model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or model:FindFirstChild("RootPart")
        or model.PrimaryPart
        or model:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
end
local function Abyss_AimPoint(model, root)
    local part = CombatAPI.TargetPart(model, root)
    if part then return part.Position, part end
    return nil, nil
end
local function Abyss_AimPointFor(inst)
    if not inst or not inst.Parent then
        return nil, nil
    end
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        local model = inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if model then
            local pos, part = Abyss_AimPoint(model, inst)
            if pos then
                return pos, part
            end
        end
        return inst.Position, inst
    end
    if inst:IsA("Model") then
        return Abyss_AimPoint(inst, Abyss_RootOf(inst))
    end
    return nil, nil
end
local Abyss_FindAimTarget = function()
    if AttackModel and AttackModel.Parent then
        return Abyss_AimPoint(AttackModel, Abyss_RootOf(AttackModel))
    end
    return nil, nil
end
local function Abyss_LiveAimPoint()
    if Abyss_AimTargetPart and Abyss_AimTargetPart.Parent then
        return Abyss_AimTargetPart.Position
    end
    return Abyss_AimPos and Abyss_AimPos.Position or nil
end
local function Abyss_SetAim(target)
    Abyss_AimPos, Abyss_AimTargetPart = nil, nil
    local pos, part
    local kind = typeof(target)
    if kind == "Instance" then
        pos, part = Abyss_AimPointFor(target)
    elseif kind == "Vector3" then
        pos = target
    elseif kind == "CFrame" then
        pos = target.Position
    end
    if not pos then
        Abyss_AimOn = false
        return nil
    end
    Abyss_AimPos = CFrame.new(pos)
    Abyss_AimTargetPart = part
    Abyss_AimOn = Abyss_AimGate() and true or false
    if Abyss_AimOn then
        Abyss_AimLastAt = os.clock()
        if not Abyss_AimHookReady and not Abyss_AimHookFailed then
            Abyss_InstallAimHook()
        end
        if not Abyss_MouseHookReady then
            Abyss_InstallMouseHook()
        end
    end
    return Abyss_AimPos
end
Abyss_AimBegin = function(target)
    Abyss_AimToken = Abyss_AimToken + 1
    if not Abyss_AimGate() then
        Abyss_AimOn = false
        return nil
    end
    if target and Abyss_SetAim(target) then
        return Abyss_AimToken
    end
    local pos, part = Abyss_FindAimTarget()
    if not pos then
        Abyss_AimPos = nil
        Abyss_AimTargetPart = nil
        Abyss_AimOn = false
        return nil
    end
    Abyss_AimPos = CFrame.new(pos)
    Abyss_AimTargetPart = part
    Abyss_AimOn = true
    Abyss_AimLastAt = os.clock()
    if not Abyss_AimHookReady and not Abyss_AimHookFailed then
        Abyss_InstallAimHook()
    end
    if not Abyss_MouseHookReady then
        Abyss_InstallMouseHook()
    end
    return Abyss_AimToken
end
Abyss_AimEndSoon = function(delay, waitCasting)
    local token = Abyss_AimToken
    task.delay(delay or 0.6, function()
        if waitCasting then
            local deadline = os.clock() + 6
            while Abyss_AimToken == token and os.clock() < deadline and CombatAPI.StillCasting() do
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end
        if Abyss_AimToken == token then
            Abyss_AimPos, Abyss_AimTargetPart = nil, nil
            Abyss_AimOn = false
        end
    end)
end
Abyss_InstallAimHook = function()
    if Abyss_AimHookReady then
        return true
    end
    if Abyss_AimHookFailed then
        return false
    end
    if type(getrawmetatable) ~= "function" or type(newcclosure) ~= "function" then
        Abyss_AimHookFailed = true
        return false
    end
    local GetMethod = getnamecallmethod
    local CheckCaller = (type(checkcaller) == "function") and checkcaller or nil
    local TypeOf = typeof
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local mt = getrawmetatable(game)
        local oldNamecall = mt.__namecall
        setreadonly(mt, false)
        mt.__namecall = newcclosure(function(self, ...)
            if not Abyss_AimOn or not Abyss_AimGate() then
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end
            if GetMethod() ~= "FireServer" then
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end
            if CheckCaller and CheckCaller() then
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end
            if self.Name ~= "RemoteEvent" or select("#", ...) ~= 1 then
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end
            local first = ...
            local kind = TypeOf(first)
            if kind ~= "Vector3" and kind ~= "CFrame" then
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end
            local pos = Abyss_LiveAimPoint()
            if not pos or pos.X ~= pos.X or pos.Y < -500 then
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end
            local replaced = (kind == "Vector3") and pos or CFrame.new(pos)
            Abyss_AimRewrites = Abyss_AimRewrites + 1
            return oldNamecall(self, replaced)
        end)
        setreadonly(mt, true)
        Abyss_AimHookMT = mt
        Abyss_AimHookOld = oldNamecall
        Abyss_AimHookReady = true
    end)
    if not ok then
        Abyss_AimHookFailed = true
        return false
    end
    return true
end
Abyss_InstallMouseHook = function()
    if Abyss_MouseHookReady then
        return true
    end
    if Abyss_AimHookFailed then
        return false
    end
    local mod = RS:FindFirstChild("Mouse")
    if not mod then
        return false
    end
    local okReq, MouseMod = pcall(require, mod)
    if not okReq or type(MouseMod) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local store = { Hit = rawget(MouseMod, "Hit"), Target = rawget(MouseMod, "Target") }
        local mt = getrawmetatable(MouseMod)
        if mt then
            setreadonly(mt, false)
        else
            mt = {}
            setmetatable(MouseMod, mt)
        end
        rawset(MouseMod, "Hit", nil)
        rawset(MouseMod, "Target", nil)
        local oldIndex = mt.__index
        local oldNewIndex = mt.__newindex
        local oldIndexIsFn = type(oldIndex) == "function"
        local oldIndexIsTable = type(oldIndex) == "table"
        local oldNewIndexIsFn = type(oldNewIndex) == "function"
        mt.__index = function(self, key)
            if key == "Hit" or key == "Target" then
                if Abyss_AimOn and Abyss_AimGate() then
                    if key == "Hit" then
                        local pos = Abyss_LiveAimPoint()
                        if pos then
                            return CFrame.new(pos)
                        end
                    elseif Abyss_AimTargetPart then
                        return Abyss_AimTargetPart
                    end
                end
                local real = store[key]
                if real ~= nil then
                    return real
                end
            end
            if oldIndexIsFn then
                return oldIndex(self, key)
            end
            if oldIndexIsTable then
                return oldIndex[key]
            end
            return store[key]
        end
        mt.__newindex = function(self, key, value)
            if key == "Hit" or key == "Target" then
                store[key] = value
            elseif oldNewIndexIsFn then
                oldNewIndex(self, key, value)
            else
                rawset(self, key, value)
            end
        end
        setreadonly(mt, true)
        Abyss_MouseMT = mt
        Abyss_MouseStore = store
        Abyss_MouseOldIndex = oldIndex
        Abyss_MouseOldNewIndex = oldNewIndex
        Abyss_MouseMod = MouseMod
    end)
    Abyss_MouseHookReady = ok
    return ok
end
local function Abyss_UninstallMouseHook()
    if not Abyss_MouseHookReady or not Abyss_MouseMT then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local mt = Abyss_MouseMT
        setreadonly(mt, false)
        mt.__index = Abyss_MouseOldIndex
        mt.__newindex = Abyss_MouseOldNewIndex
        setreadonly(mt, true)
        local mod = RS:FindFirstChild("Mouse")
        local loaded, MouseMod = pcall(require, mod)
        if loaded and type(MouseMod) == "table" and Abyss_MouseStore then
            rawset(MouseMod, "Hit", Abyss_MouseStore.Hit)
            rawset(MouseMod, "Target", Abyss_MouseStore.Target)
        end
    end)
    Abyss_MouseHookReady = false
    Abyss_MouseMT = nil
    Abyss_MouseMod = nil
end
local function Abyss_UninstallAimHook()
    Abyss_UninstallMouseHook()
    if not Abyss_AimHookReady or not Abyss_AimHookMT then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        setreadonly(Abyss_AimHookMT, false)
        Abyss_AimHookMT.__namecall = Abyss_AimHookOld
        setreadonly(Abyss_AimHookMT, true)
    end)
    Abyss_AimHookReady = false
    Abyss_AimHookMT = nil
    Abyss_AimHookOld = nil
end
_G.Abyss_AimInstall = function()
    Abyss_AimHookFailed = false
    local eventOk = Abyss_InstallAimHook()
    local mouseOk = Abyss_InstallMouseHook()
    if not eventOk then
        print("[Aim] este executor no permite getrawmetatable: no se puede interceptar los remotes")
    end
    return eventOk, mouseOk
end
_G.Abyss_AimUninstall = Abyss_UninstallAimHook
_G.Abyss_AimStats = function()
    print(string.format("[Aim] remotes reescritos: %d | objetivo: %s | ventana: %s | hook: %s",
        Abyss_AimRewrites,
        Abyss_AimTargetPart and Abyss_AimTargetPart.Name or "ninguno",
        Abyss_AimOn and "abierta" or "cerrada",
        Abyss_AimHookReady and "on" or "off"))
end
task.spawn(function()
    if _G.SilentAim then
        _G.Abyss_AimInstall()
    end
    while task.wait(1) do
        if (Abyss_AimHookReady or Abyss_MouseHookReady) and not Abyss_AimOn
            and os.clock() - Abyss_AimLastAt > 3 then
            Abyss_UninstallAimHook()
        end
    end
end)
end
local function Update_Character()
    Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    HD = Character and (Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid"))
    HRP = Character and (Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    if _G.AutoLeviathanFull and _G.Mode == "Capitán" then
        pcall(function()
            local boats = workspace:FindFirstChild("Boats")
            if boats then
                local Marked = 0
                for _, boat in ipairs(boats:GetChildren()) do
                    if IsBeastHunterBoat(boat) and BoatOwnerMatch(boat, LocalPlayer) then
                        _G.Abyss_AbandonedBoats[boat] = true
                        Marked = Marked + 1
                    end
                end
                if Marked > 0 then
                    print("[Abyss] El capitán murió: abandonados " .. Marked .. " barcos viejos, pendientes de recompra")
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    task.wait(1)
    Update_Character()
    task.wait(2)
    Haki_Auto()
end)
LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    Character = nil
    HD = nil
    HRP = nil
    NoclipOn = false
    LastNoclipApply = 0
end)
local CraftStatus = "Desactivado"
local CraftQualityList = {
    { Key = "CommonScroll", Label = "Common Scroll" },
    { Key = "RareScroll", Label = "Rare Scroll" },
    { Key = "LegendaryScroll", Label = "Legendary Scroll" },
    { Key = "MythicalScroll", Label = "Mythical Scroll" },
}
local AUTO_CRAFT_BOTH_LABEL = "Mythical + Legendary"
local function CountValue(value)
    if type(value) == "table" then
        value = value.Quantity or value.Count or value.Amount or value.Value
    end
    return tonumber(value)
end
function CraftQualityList.GetLabel(Key)
    for _, scroll in ipairs(CraftQualityList) do
        if scroll.Key == Key then
            return scroll.Label
        end
    end
end
function CraftQualityList.GetKey(KeyOrLabel)
    if KeyOrLabel == nil then
        return nil
    end
    local normalized = tostring(KeyOrLabel):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("_", "")
    for _, scroll in ipairs(CraftQualityList) do
        local key = tostring(scroll.Key):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("_", "")
        local label = tostring(scroll.Label):lower():gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("_", "")
        if normalized == key or normalized == label then
            return scroll.Key
        end
    end
end
function CraftQualityList.Lookup(source, Key)
    if not source then
        return nil
    end
    local canonical = CraftQualityList.GetKey(Key) or Key
    local value = CountValue(source[canonical])
    if value == nil then
        local label = CraftQualityList.GetLabel(canonical) or tostring(Key)
        value = CountValue(source[label])
    end
    return value
end
function CraftQualityList.GetCount(Prog, Etc, Key)
    local canonical = CraftQualityList.GetKey(Key) or Key
    local preferred = (canonical == "MythicalScroll") and Etc or Prog
    local fallback = (preferred == Etc) and Prog or Etc
    local value = CraftQualityList.Lookup(preferred, canonical)
    if value == nil then
        value = CraftQualityList.Lookup(fallback, canonical)
    end
    return value or 0
end
function CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(Etc, Key)
    -- Cuenta real de pergaminos en el inventario (EtcItems). Nunca usa la progresion
    -- (CraftProgression), que acumula lo fabricado y daba 10/20 sin tenerlos.
    if type(Etc) ~= "table" then
        return 0
    end
    return CraftQualityList.Lookup(Etc, Key) or 0
end
function CraftQualityList.GetProgressView(Prog, Etc)
    local view = {}
    for key, value in pairs(Prog or {}) do
        view[key] = value
    end
    for _, scroll in ipairs(CraftQualityList) do
        local count = CraftQualityList.GetCount(Prog, Etc, scroll.Key)
        view[scroll.Key] = count
        view[scroll.Label] = count
    end
    return view
end
local function NormalizeKey(value)
    return (tostring(value or ""):lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", ""))
end
-- Busca una cantidad tolerando diferencias de nombre entre las tablas del juego
-- ("LegendaryScroll", "Legendary Scroll", "legendary_scroll", etc.).
local function LookupCount(source, key)
    if type(source) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end
    local value = CountValue(source[key])
    if value ~= nil then
        return value
    end
    local canonical = CraftQualityList.GetKey(key)
    if canonical then
        value = CraftQualityList.Lookup(source, canonical)
        if value ~= nil then
            return value
        end
    end
    local wanted = NormalizeKey(key)
    for sourceKey, rawValue in pairs(source) do
        if NormalizeKey(sourceKey) == wanted then
            local count = CountValue(rawValue)
            if count ~= nil then
                return count
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function GetProgressCount(Prog, Etc, key)
    local value = LookupCount(Prog, key)
    -- Los pergaminos tambien cuentan si estan en el inventario: la progresion del
    -- juego no siempre registra los misticos y bloqueaba los legendarios.
    if CraftQualityList.GetKey(key) then
        local stock = LookupCount(Etc, key)
        if stock ~= nil and (value == nil or stock > value) then
            value = stock
        end
    end
    if value == nil then
        value = LookupCount(Etc, key)
    end
    return value or 0
end
local function AsRecipeTable(value)
    if type(value) == "table" then
        return value
    end
    return nil
end
local function GetRecipeProgress(recipe)
    return AsRecipeTable(recipe.ProgressionRequirement)
        or AsRecipeTable(recipe.ProgressionRequirements)
        or AsRecipeTable(recipe.ProgressRequirement)
        or {}
end
local function GetRecipeIngredients(recipe)
    return AsRecipeTable(recipe.Ingredients)
        or AsRecipeTable(recipe.Materials)
        or AsRecipeTable(recipe.Items)
        or AsRecipeTable(recipe.Cost)
        or {}
end
local function FindRecipe(Recipes, key)
    if type(Recipes) ~= "table" then
        return nil, key
    end
    if type(Recipes[key]) == "table" then
        return Recipes[key], key, key
    end
    local canonical = CraftQualityList.GetKey(key)
    if canonical and canonical ~= key and type(Recipes[canonical]) == "table" then
        return Recipes[canonical], canonical, canonical
    end
    local wanted = NormalizeKey(key)
    for recipeKey, recipe in pairs(Recipes) do
        if type(recipe) == "table" then
            local candidates = { recipeKey, recipe.Key, recipe.Id, recipe.Name, recipe.Quality }
            for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
                if candidate ~= nil and NormalizeKey(candidate) == wanted then
                    return recipe, (CraftQualityList.GetKey(candidate) or canonical or key), recipeKey
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil, key
end
function CraftQualityList.GetNextUnlockKey(Recipes, Prog, Etc, Key, Depth, Seen)
    Depth = Depth or 0
    Seen = Seen or {}
    if Depth > #CraftQualityList + 2 then
        return nil, "depth"
    end
    local canonical = CraftQualityList.GetKey(Key) or Key
    if Seen[canonical] then
        -- Cadena circular (por ejemplo Legendary <-> Mythical): no se puede
        -- resolver sola, el llamador intenta la receta destino y muestra
        -- exactamente que requisito falta.
        return nil, "cycle", canonical
    end
    Seen[canonical] = true
    local recipe = FindRecipe(Recipes, canonical)
    if not recipe then
        Seen[canonical] = nil
        return nil, "recipe", canonical
    end
    for progressKey, required in pairs(GetRecipeProgress(recipe)) do
        local dependency = CraftQualityList.GetKey(progressKey)
        local current = GetProgressCount(Prog, Etc, progressKey)
        local needed = tonumber(required) or 0
        if current < needed then
            if not dependency then
                Seen[canonical] = nil
                return nil, "progress", progressKey, current, needed
            end
            local stepKey, stepError, stepDetail, stepCurrent, stepRequired =
                CraftQualityList.GetNextUnlockKey(Recipes, Prog, Etc, dependency, Depth + 1, Seen)
            if stepKey then
                Seen[canonical] = nil
                return stepKey, stepError, stepDetail, stepCurrent, stepRequired
            end
            if stepError ~= "cycle" then
                Seen[canonical] = nil
                return nil, stepError, stepDetail, stepCurrent, stepRequired
            end
            -- Ciclo con este requisito: se prueba con el siguiente.
        end
    end
    for ingredientKey, required in pairs(GetRecipeIngredients(recipe)) do
        if type(ingredientKey) == "string" then
            local dependency = CraftQualityList.GetKey(ingredientKey)
            local current = dependency and (LookupCount(Etc, dependency) or 0) or nil
            local needed = tonumber(required) or 0
            if dependency and current < needed then
                local stepKey, stepError, stepDetail, stepCurrent, stepRequired =
                    CraftQualityList.GetNextUnlockKey(Recipes, Prog, Etc, dependency, Depth + 1, Seen)
                if stepKey then
                    Seen[canonical] = nil
                    return stepKey, stepError, stepDetail, stepCurrent, stepRequired
                end
                if stepError ~= "cycle" then
                    Seen[canonical] = nil
                    return nil, stepError, stepDetail, stepCurrent, stepRequired
                end
                -- Ciclo con este ingrediente: se prueba con el siguiente.
            end
        end
    end
    Seen[canonical] = nil
    return canonical
end
local CraftRecipesCache = nil
local function GetCraftRecipes()
    if not CraftRecipesCache then
        local ok, r = pcall(function()
            return require(RS.Modules.Data.CraftingRecipes)
        end)
        if ok then
            CraftRecipesCache = r
        end
    end
    return CraftRecipesCache
end
local function GetCraftData()
    local RF = RS.Modules and RS.Modules:FindFirstChild("Net") and RS.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/GetCraftPlayerData")
    if not RF then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        return RF:InvokeServer()
    end)
    if ok and type(data) == "table" then
        return data.EtcItems, data.CraftProgression
    end
    return nil, nil
end
local function CheckCraftRecipe(r, Etc, Prog)
    for k, v in pairs(GetRecipeProgress(r)) do
        local current = GetProgressCount(Prog, Etc, k)
        local required = tonumber(v) or 0
        if current < required then
            return false, TranslateStatus("Falta progreso " .. tostring(k) .. " (" .. current .. "/" .. required .. ")")
        end
    end
    for k, v in pairs(GetRecipeIngredients(r)) do
        if type(k) == "string" then
            local current = LookupCount(Etc, k) or 0
            local required = tonumber(v) or 0
            if current < required then
                return false, TranslateStatus("Falta " .. k .. " (" .. current .. "/" .. required .. ")")
            end
        end
    end
    return true
end
local CRAFT_NPC_POS = Vector3.new(-15906.6494, 483.391998, 945.215027)
local function NearCraftNPC(range)
    local _, hrp = GetCharacterParts()
    if not hrp then
        return false
    end
    return (hrp.Position - CRAFT_NPC_POS).Magnitude <= (range or 80)
end

local function TotalEtcCount(Etc)
    local total = 0
    if type(Etc) ~= "table" then
        return total
    end
    for _, value in pairs(Etc) do
        local count = CountValue(value)
        if count then
            total = total + count
        end
    end
    return total
end

-- The game remote signature changed between updates. Try one signature per
-- attempt and verify the inventory; never report a craft that did not happen.
local CRAFT_SIGNATURES = {
    { "Craft", "__KEY__", 1, {} },
    { "__KEY__", 1, {} },
    { "Craft", "__KEY__", 1 },
    { "__KEY__", 1 },
    { "Craft", "__KEY__" },
    { "__KEY__" },
    { { Recipe = "__KEY__", Amount = 1 } },
    { "Craft", { Recipe = "__KEY__", Amount = 1 } },
    { "__KEY__", { Amount = 1 } },
    { "Craft", "__KEY__", { Amount = 1 } },
}
local function ReplaceCraftKey(value, key)
    if type(value) == "table" then
        local out = {}
        for k, v in pairs(value) do
            out[k] = ReplaceCraftKey(v, key)
        end
        return out
    end
    if value == "__KEY__" then
        return key
    end
    return value
end
local function BuildCraftArgs(signature, key)
    local args = {}
    for i, value in ipairs(signature) do
        args[i] = ReplaceCraftKey(value, key)
    end
    return args
end
function TryCraft(key, targetKey)
    local RF = RS.Modules and RS.Modules:FindFirstChild("Net") and RS.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/Craft")
    if not RF then
        return "noremote"
    end
    local beforeEtc, beforeProg = GetCraftData()
    if type(beforeEtc) ~= "table" then
        return "nodata"
    end
    local checkKey = targetKey or key
    local beforeInv = CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(beforeEtc, key)
    local beforeTarget = CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(beforeEtc, checkKey)
    local beforeProgress = CraftQualityList.GetCount(beforeProg, beforeEtc, key)
    local beforeTotal = TotalEtcCount(beforeEtc)

    local sigIndex = tonumber(_G.Abyss_CraftSignature) or 1
    if sigIndex < 1 or sigIndex > #CRAFT_SIGNATURES then
        sigIndex = 1
    end
    local args = BuildCraftArgs(CRAFT_SIGNATURES[sigIndex], key)
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return RF:InvokeServer(unpack(args))
    end)
    if ok then
        task.wait(1.0)
        local afterEtc, afterProg = GetCraftData()
        if type(afterEtc) == "table" then
            local afterInv = CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(afterEtc, key)
            local afterTarget = CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(afterEtc, checkKey)
            local afterProgress = CraftQualityList.GetCount(afterProg, afterEtc, key)
            local afterTotal = TotalEtcCount(afterEtc)
            if afterInv > beforeInv or afterTarget > beforeTarget then
                _G.Abyss_CraftSignature = sigIndex
                return "ok", sigIndex
            end
            if afterProgress > beforeProgress then
                _G.Abyss_CraftSignature = sigIndex
                return "progress", sigIndex
            end
            if afterTotal < beforeTotal then
                _G.Abyss_CraftSignature = sigIndex
                return "consumed", sigIndex
            end
        end
        if result ~= nil and result ~= false then
            _G.Abyss_CraftSignature = sigIndex
            return "accepted", sigIndex
        end
    end
    -- No inventory/progression change: try the next signature next time.
    _G.Abyss_CraftSignature = (sigIndex % #CRAFT_SIGNATURES) + 1
    return "rejected", sigIndex
end
-- Fabrica un paso hacia la calidad elegida. Si la receta destino todavia no se
-- puede fabricar (por ejemplo Legendary necesita Mythical), fabrica primero el
-- requisito que falta en lugar de quedarse bloqueado.
local function CraftStep(quality)
    local Etc, Prog = GetCraftData()
    local Recipes = GetCraftRecipes()
    if not Etc or not Prog or not Recipes then
        return T("craft.status.error")
    end
    local targetKey = CraftQualityList.GetKey(quality) or quality
    local targetLabel = CraftQualityList.GetLabel(targetKey) or tostring(quality)
    local cap = 0
    if targetKey == "LegendaryScroll" then
        cap = 20
    elseif targetKey == "MythicalScroll" then
        cap = 10
    end
    if cap > 0 and CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(Etc, targetKey) >= cap then
        local full = TranslateStatus(targetLabel .. ": stacks full (" .. cap .. ")")
        -- Pila llena: apaga Auto Craft en vez de reintentar cada 5 segundos.
        _G.AutoCraftScrolls = false
        SaveConfig()
        if _G.Abyss_AutoCraftScrollsToggle then
            pcall(function()
                _G.Abyss_AutoCraftScrollsToggle:SetValue(false)
            end)
        end
        CraftQualityList.StopReason = full
        return full
    end
    local craftKey, chainError, detail, current, required =
        CraftQualityList.GetNextUnlockKey(Recipes, Prog, Etc, targetKey)
    if not craftKey then
        if chainError == "cycle" then
            -- Dependencias cruzadas: se intenta la receta destino y
            -- CheckCraftRecipe indica el requisito que falta.
            craftKey = targetKey
        elseif chainError == "progress" then
            return TranslateStatus("Falta progreso " .. tostring(detail) .. " (" .. tostring(current) .. "/" .. tostring(required) .. ")")
        elseif chainError == "recipe" then
            return TranslateStatus("Receta no encontrada: " .. tostring(detail))
        else
            return T("craft.status.error")
        end
    end
    local recipe, recipeKey, recipeTableKey = FindRecipe(Recipes, craftKey)
    if not recipe then
        return TranslateStatus("Receta no encontrada: " .. tostring(craftKey))
    end
    local label = CraftQualityList.GetLabel(craftKey) or CraftQualityList.GetLabel(recipeKey) or tostring(craftKey)
    local canCraft, missing = CheckCraftRecipe(recipe, Etc, Prog)
    if not canCraft then
        return TranslateStatus(label .. ": " .. missing)
    end
    local actualKey = recipeTableKey or CraftQualityList.GetKey(recipeKey) or craftKey
    local result, detail = TryCraft(actualKey, targetKey)
    if result == "ok" then
        return TranslateStatus(label .. ": fabricado (confirmado)")
    elseif result == "progress" or result == "consumed" or result == "accepted" then
        _G.AutoCraftScrolls = false
        SaveConfig()
        if _G.Abyss_AutoCraftScrollsToggle then
            pcall(function()
                _G.Abyss_AutoCraftScrollsToggle:SetValue(false)
            end)
        end
        local reason
        if result == "progress" then
            reason = "progreso aumentado"
        elseif result == "consumed" then
            reason = "materiales consumidos"
        else
            reason = "el servidor acepto el craft"
        end
        CraftQualityList.StopReason = TranslateStatus(label .. ": " .. reason .. ", pero el pergamino no aparecio en el inventario; auto craft detenido")
        return CraftQualityList.StopReason
    elseif result == "noremote" then
        return TranslateStatus("No se encontro el remote RF/Craft")
    elseif result == "nodata" then
        return TranslateStatus("No se pudo leer el inventario antes del craft")
    end
    return TranslateStatus(label .. ": sin confirmar; probando otra firma del remote (" .. tostring(detail or "?") .. ")")
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(5)
        local Q = _G.CraftQuality
        if not _G.AutoUnlockMythical then
            if not _G.AutoCraftScrolls or Q == nil or Q == "None" then
                if CraftQualityList.StopReason then
                    CraftStatus = CraftQualityList.StopReason
                elseif CraftStatus ~= T("craft.status.none") then
                    CraftStatus = T("craft.status.none")
                end
            else
                CraftQualityList.StopReason = nil
                local ok, status = pcall(function()
                    local quality = Q
                    if quality == AUTO_CRAFT_BOTH_LABEL then
                        local Etc = GetCraftData()
                        local mythic = (type(Etc) == "table")
                            and CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(Etc, "MythicalScroll") or 0
                        -- Etapa persistente: se llena Mythical una vez y despues
                        -- se apunta siempre a Legendary. CraftStep ya fabrica los
                        -- requisitos (Mythical) cuando faltan, asi el bucle no
                        -- salta entre calidades y se queda sin avanzar.
                        if CraftQualityList.BothStage ~= "Legendary" and mythic < 10 then
                            quality = "Mythical Scroll"
                        else
                            CraftQualityList.BothStage = "Legendary"
                            quality = "Legendary Scroll"
                        end
                    end
                    return CraftStep(quality)
                end)
                if ok and type(status) == "string" then
                    CraftStatus = status
                elseif not ok then
                    CraftStatus = T("craft.status.error")
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
local function StopAutoUnlockMythical(statusKey)
    _G.AutoUnlockMythical = false
    CraftQualityList.UnlockStage = nil
    SaveConfig()
    if _G.Abyss_AutoUnlockMythicalToggle then
        pcall(function()
            _G.Abyss_AutoUnlockMythicalToggle:SetValue(false)
        end)
    end
    CraftStatus = T(statusKey)
end
local function AutoUnlockTargetKey()
    local Q = _G.CraftQuality
    if Q == "Legendary Scroll" then
        return "LegendaryScroll"
    elseif Q == AUTO_CRAFT_BOTH_LABEL then
        if CraftQualityList.UnlockStage == "Legendary" then
            return "LegendaryScroll"
        end
        return "MythicalScroll"
    end
    return "MythicalScroll"
end
function AutoUnlockMythicalStep()
    local Etc, Prog = GetCraftData()
    local Recipes = GetCraftRecipes()
    if not Etc or not Prog or not Recipes then
        CraftStatus = T("craft.status.error")
        return
    end
    if CraftQualityList.Pending then
        local pending = CraftQualityList.Pending
        local current = CraftQualityList.GetCount(Prog, Etc, pending.Key)
        local quality = _G.CraftQuality
        local bothSelected = quality == AUTO_CRAFT_BOTH_LABEL
        if current > pending.Before then
            CraftQualityList.Pending = nil
            if pending.Key == "MythicalScroll" or pending.Key == "LegendaryScroll" then
                local pendingLabel = CraftQualityList.GetLabel(pending.Key) or pending.Key
                if bothSelected and pending.Key == "MythicalScroll"
                    and CraftQualityList.UnlockStage ~= "Legendary" then
                    CraftQualityList.UnlockStage = "Legendary"
                    CraftQualityList.BothStage = "Legendary"
                    CraftStatus = TranslateStatus(pendingLabel .. ": fabricado; continuando con Legendary Scroll")
                    return
                end
                local keepCrafting = _G.AutoCraftScrolls and (bothSelected or quality == pendingLabel)
                StopAutoUnlockMythical("craft.unlock.done")
                if keepCrafting then
                    _G.AutoCraftScrolls = true
                    if _G.Abyss_AutoCraftScrollsToggle then
                        pcall(function()
                            _G.Abyss_AutoCraftScrollsToggle:SetValue(true)
                        end)
                    else
                        SaveConfig()
                    end
                end
                return
            end
        elseif os.clock() - pending.At < 15 then
            CraftStatus = T("craft.unlock.wait") .. ": " .. pending.Label
            return
        else
            CraftQualityList.Pending = nil
            if (pending.Key == "MythicalScroll" or pending.Key == "LegendaryScroll") and pending.Accepted then
                if bothSelected and pending.Key == "MythicalScroll"
                    and CraftQualityList.UnlockStage ~= "Legendary" then
                    CraftQualityList.UnlockStage = "Legendary"
                    CraftQualityList.BothStage = "Legendary"
                    CraftStatus = TranslateStatus("Mythical Scroll not confirmed; continuing with Legendary Scroll")
                    return
                end
                StopAutoUnlockMythical("craft.unlock.unknown")
                return
            end
        end
    end
    local unlockTarget = AutoUnlockTargetKey()
    local unlockCount = CraftQualityList.GetInventoryCount(Etc, unlockTarget)
    local unlockCap = (unlockTarget == "LegendaryScroll") and 20 or 10
    if unlockCount >= unlockCap then
        if _G.CraftQuality == AUTO_CRAFT_BOTH_LABEL and unlockTarget == "MythicalScroll" then
            CraftQualityList.UnlockStage = "Legendary"
            CraftQualityList.BothStage = "Legendary"
            return
        end
        StopAutoUnlockMythical("craft.unlock.done")
        return
    end
    local craftKey, unlockError, detail, current, required =
        CraftQualityList.GetNextUnlockKey(Recipes, Prog, Etc, unlockTarget)
    if not craftKey then
        if unlockError == "cycle" then
            -- Dependencias cruzadas: se intenta el pergamino objetivo.
            craftKey = unlockTarget
        elseif unlockError == "progress" then
            CraftStatus = TranslateStatus(
                "Falta progreso " .. tostring(detail) .. " (" .. tostring(current) .. "/" .. tostring(required) .. ")"
            )
        elseif unlockError == "ingredient" then
            CraftStatus = TranslateStatus("Falta " .. tostring(detail) .. " (" .. tostring(current) .. "/" .. tostring(required) .. ")")
        elseif unlockError == "recipe" then
            CraftStatus = TranslateStatus("Receta no encontrada: " .. tostring(detail))
        else
            CraftStatus = T("craft.status.error")
        end
        return
    end
    local recipe, recipeKey, recipeTableKey = FindRecipe(Recipes, craftKey)
    if not recipe then
        CraftStatus = TranslateStatus("Receta no encontrada: " .. craftKey)
        return
    end
    local canCraft, missing = CheckCraftRecipe(recipe, Etc, Prog)
    if not canCraft then
        CraftStatus = T("craft.unlock") .. ": " .. missing
        return
    end
    local label = CraftQualityList.GetLabel(craftKey) or craftKey
    local beforeCount = CraftQualityList.GetCount(Prog, Etc, craftKey)
    local result, detail = TryCraft(recipeTableKey or CraftQualityList.GetKey(recipeKey) or craftKey, unlockTarget)
    if result == "ok" then
        CraftQualityList.Pending = {
            Key = craftKey,
            Before = beforeCount,
            At = os.clock(),
            Label = label,
            Accepted = true,
        }
        CraftStatus = T("craft.unlock") .. ": " .. label
    elseif result == "progress" or result == "consumed" or result == "accepted" then
        _G.AutoUnlockMythical = false
        CraftQualityList.UnlockStage = nil
        CraftQualityList.Pending = nil
        SaveConfig()
        if _G.Abyss_AutoUnlockMythicalToggle then
            pcall(function()
                _G.Abyss_AutoUnlockMythicalToggle:SetValue(false)
            end)
        end
        local reason
        if result == "progress" then
            reason = "progreso aumentado"
        elseif result == "consumed" then
            reason = "materiales consumidos"
        else
            reason = "el servidor acepto el craft"
        end
        CraftStatus = TranslateStatus(label .. ": " .. reason .. ", pero el pergamino no aparecio en el inventario; auto unlock detenido")
        CraftQualityList.StopReason = CraftStatus
    else
        CraftStatus = T("craft.unlock.retry") .. ": " .. label .. " (firma " .. tostring(detail or "?") .. ")"
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(2)
        if _G.AutoUnlockMythical then
            local ok, err = pcall(AutoUnlockMythicalStep)
            if not ok then
                print("[DEBUG] AutoUnlockMythicalStep: " .. tostring(err))
                StopAutoUnlockMythical("craft.status.error")
            end
        end
    end
end)
local FruitStoreBlackList = {}
local FruitStoreFailCount = {}
local function FruitCommF()
    if CommF and CommF.Parent then
        return CommF
    end
    local remotes = RS:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    return remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("CommF_")
end
local function GachaPull()
    local modules = RS:FindFirstChild("Modules")
    local net = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Net")
    local gacha = net and net:FindFirstChild("RF/GachaNetworkRF")
    if not gacha then
        return false
    end
    pcall(function()
        gacha:InvokeServer({ Context = "Purchase", BoxName = "ZiolesGacha" })
    end)
    return true
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(120)
        if _G.AutoRandomFruit and HRP and HD and HD.Health > 0 then
            GachaPull()
        end
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        if _G.AutoStoreFruits and HRP then
            local containers = { LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack"), LocalPlayer.Character }
            for _, container in ipairs(containers) do
                if container then
                    for _, tool in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        if tool:IsA("Tool") and string.find(tool.Name, "Fruit", 1, true) then
                            local original = tool:GetAttribute("OriginalName")
                            local key = original or tool.Name
                            if not FruitStoreBlackList[key] then
                                local home = tool.Parent
                                local comm = FruitCommF()
                                if comm then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        comm:InvokeServer("StoreFruit", key, tool)
                                    end)
                                end
                                task.wait(0.2)
                                if tool.Parent == home then
                                    local fails = (FruitStoreFailCount[key] or 0) + 1
                                    FruitStoreFailCount[key] = fails
                                    if fails >= 3 then
                                        FruitStoreBlackList[key] = true
                                    end
                                else
                                    FruitStoreFailCount[key] = nil
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
local _ = (function()
do
    local LegacyCaptain = "\229\188\128\232\136\185"
    local LegacyFollower = "\232\183\159\233\154\143"
    if _G.Mode == LegacyCaptain then
        _G.Mode = "Capitán"
    elseif _G.Mode == LegacyFollower then
        _G.Mode = "Seguidor"
    end
end
-- Fluent trae varios temas. Se dejan solo dos, en este orden:
-- "Dark" (gris comun con acento del logo) y "Abyss Original" (todos los tonos
-- derivados de la imagen). "Dark" queda por defecto.
local function PatchFluentTheme(source)
    if type(source) ~= "string" then
        return source
    end
    local function rgb(c)
        return ("Color3.fromRGB(%d,%d,%d)"):format(c[1], c[2], c[3])
    end
    local theme = table.concat({
        "Name='Dark'",
        "Accent=" .. rgb(AbyssBaseRGB),
        "AcrylicMain=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelRGB),
        "AcrylicBorder=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "AcrylicGradient=ColorSequence.new(" .. rgb(AbyssPanelDeepRGB) .. "," .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB) .. ")",
        "AcrylicNoise=0.9",
        "TitleBarLine=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "Tab=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelSoftRGB),
        "Element=" .. rgb(AbyssElementRGB),
        "ElementBorder=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "InElementBorder=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "ElementTransparency=0.72",
        "ToggleSlider=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelLightRGB),
        "ToggleToggled=" .. rgb(AbyssLineRGB),
        "SliderRail=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "DropdownFrame=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelRGB),
        "DropdownHolder=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelSoftRGB),
        "DropdownBorder=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "DropdownOption=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelDeepRGB),
        "Keybind=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelRGB),
        "Input=" .. rgb(AbyssElementRGB),
        "InputFocused=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelSoftRGB),
        "InputIndicator=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "Dialog=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelDeepRGB),
        "DialogHolder=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelSoftRGB),
        "DialogHolderLine=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "DialogButton=" .. rgb(AbyssPanelRGB),
        "DialogButtonBorder=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "DialogBorder=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "DialogInput=" .. rgb(AbyssElementRGB),
        "DialogInputLine=" .. rgb(AbyssAmberRGB),
        "Text=" .. rgb(AbyssInkRGB),
        "SubText=" .. rgb(AbyssMutedRGB),
        "Hover=" .. rgb(AbyssGray(255)),
        "HoverChange=0.12}",
    }, ",")

    -- Tonos del tema anterior, calculados desde el mismo color del logo.
    local function logoShade(f)
        return {
            math.clamp(math.floor(AbyssBaseRGB[1] * f + 0.5), 0, 255),
            math.clamp(math.floor(AbyssBaseRGB[2] * f + 0.5), 0, 255),
            math.clamp(math.floor(AbyssBaseRGB[3] * f + 0.5), 0, 255),
        }
    end
    local function logoTint(a)
        return {
            math.clamp(math.floor(AbyssBaseRGB[1] + (255 - AbyssBaseRGB[1]) * a + 0.5), 0, 255),
            math.clamp(math.floor(AbyssBaseRGB[2] + (255 - AbyssBaseRGB[2]) * a + 0.5), 0, 255),
            math.clamp(math.floor(AbyssBaseRGB[3] + (255 - AbyssBaseRGB[3]) * a + 0.5), 0, 255),
        }
    end
    local LAccent = AbyssBaseRGB
    local LInk = logoShade(0.18)
    local LAmber = logoShade(0.55)
    local LPanel = logoTint(0.72)
    local LPanelSoft = logoTint(0.82)
    local LPanelDeep = logoTint(0.62)
    local LElement = logoTint(0.85)
    local LPanelLight = logoTint(0.88)
    local LLine = logoShade(0.75)
    local LMuted = logoShade(0.45)
    local logoTheme = table.concat({
        "Name='Abyss Original'",
        "Accent=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "AcrylicMain=" .. rgb(LAccent),
        "AcrylicBorder=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "AcrylicGradient=ColorSequence.new(" .. rgb(LPanelSoft) .. "," .. rgb(LPanelDeep) .. ")",
        "AcrylicNoise=0.9",
        "TitleBarLine=" .. rgb(LLine),
        "Tab=" .. rgb(LPanelSoft),
        "Element=" .. rgb(LElement),
        "ElementBorder=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "InElementBorder=" .. rgb(LLine),
        "ElementTransparency=0.72",
        "ToggleSlider=" .. rgb(LAccent),
        "ToggleToggled=" .. rgb(LPanelLight),
        "SliderRail=" .. rgb(LLine),
        "DropdownFrame=" .. rgb(LPanel),
        "DropdownHolder=" .. rgb(LPanelSoft),
        "DropdownBorder=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "DropdownOption=" .. rgb(LPanelDeep),
        "Keybind=" .. rgb(LPanel),
        "Input=" .. rgb(LElement),
        "InputFocused=" .. rgb(LPanelLight),
        "InputIndicator=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "Dialog=" .. rgb(LPanelDeep),
        "DialogHolder=" .. rgb(LPanelSoft),
        "DialogHolderLine=" .. rgb(LLine),
        "DialogButton=" .. rgb(LPanel),
        "DialogButtonBorder=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "DialogBorder=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "DialogInput=" .. rgb(LElement),
        "DialogInputLine=" .. rgb(LAmber),
        "Text=" .. rgb(LInk),
        "SubText=" .. rgb(LMuted),
        "Hover=" .. rgb(LPanelLight),
        "HoverChange=0.12}",
    }, ",")

    local patched, count = source:gsub("Name='Dark',.-HoverChange=0%.07}", theme)
    if count ~= 1 then
        warn("[Abyss] No se pudo aplicar el tema Dark a Fluent (coincidencias: " .. tostring(count) .. ").")
        return source
    end
    local logoCount
    patched, logoCount = patched:gsub("Name='Darker',.-DialogInputLine=Color3%.fromRGB%(120,120,120%)%}", logoTheme)
    local hasLogo = logoCount == 1
    _G.AbyssHasLogoTheme = hasLogo
    if not hasLogo then
        warn("[Abyss] No se pudo aplicar el tema Abyss Original a Fluent (coincidencias: " .. tostring(logoCount) .. "). Se usa solo Dark.")
    end
    -- El selector muestra solo los temas que realmente existen, en este orden.
    local listCount
    if hasLogo then
        patched, listCount = patched:gsub("Names={'Dark',.-}", "Names={'Dark','Abyss Original'}", 1)
    else
        patched, listCount = patched:gsub("Names={'Dark',.-}", "Names={'Dark'}", 1)
    end
    if listCount ~= 1 then
        warn("[Abyss] No se pudo limitar la lista de temas de Fluent.")
    end
    -- La sombra de la ventana es una imagen negra casi opaca que apaga el panel.
    -- Se hace mas transparente para que el panel gris se vea limpio.
    patched = patched:gsub("ImageColor3=Color3%.fromRGB%(0,0,0%),ImageTransparency=0%.7",
        "ImageColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),ImageTransparency=0.9", 1)
    local chunk = loadstring(patched)
    if type(chunk) ~= "function" then
        warn("[Abyss] Los temas Abyss generaron un error de sintaxis; se usa el tema original.")
        return source
    end
    return patched
end

local function PatchInterfaceManager(source)
    if type(source) ~= "string" then
        return source
    end
    -- El tema por defecto sigue siendo "Dark" (el primero del selector).
    local patched = source
    -- Solo se aceptan los temas que existen; un options.json antiguo no puede
    -- restaurar los eliminados, pero si puede recordar el tema elegido.
    local extra = _G.AbyssHasLogoTheme and " or v == 'Abyss Original'" or ""
    patched = patched:gsub("for i, v in next, decoded do%s-InterfaceManager%.Settings%[i%] = v%s-end",
        "for i, v in next, decoded do if i ~= 'Theme' or v == 'Dark'" .. extra .. " then InterfaceManager.Settings[i] = v end end", 1)
    return patched
end

local function LoadRemote(url, label, tries)
    tries = tries or 4
    local lastErr = "desconocido"
    for attempt = 1, tries do
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            local body = game:HttpGet(url)
            if type(body) ~= "string" or #body < 64 then
                error("respuesta vacia o invalida")
            end
            if label == "Fluent" then
                local okPatch, patched = pcall(PatchFluentTheme, body)
                if okPatch and type(patched) == "string" then
                    body = patched
                else
                    -- Un fallo del parche nunca debe impedir cargar la libreria.
                    _G.AbyssHasLogoTheme = false
                    warn("[Abyss] Parche de temas omitido: " .. tostring(patched))
                end
            elseif label == "InterfaceManager" then
                local okPatch, patched = pcall(PatchInterfaceManager, body)
                if okPatch and type(patched) == "string" then
                    body = patched
                else
                    warn("[Abyss] Parche de InterfaceManager omitido: " .. tostring(patched))
                end
            end
            local chunk, compileErr = loadstring(body)
            if not chunk then
                error(compileErr or "loadstring fallo")
            end
            return chunk()
        end)
        if ok and result ~= nil then
            return result
        end
        lastErr = result
        if attempt < tries then
            warn(("[Abyss] Reintentando %s (%d/%d): %s"):format(tostring(label), attempt, tries, tostring(result)))
            task.wait(0.4 * attempt)
        end
    end
    warn(("[Abyss] No se pudo cargar %s: %s"):format(tostring(label), tostring(lastErr)))
    return nil
end

print("[Abyss] Cargando libreria de interfaz (Fluent)...")

local LANGUAGE_LABELS = _G.Abyss_LanguageLabels or { "English", "Español" }
local function NormalizeLanguage(value)
    local norm = _G.Abyss_NormalizeLanguage
    if norm then
        return norm(value)
    end
    return value
end
_G.Language = NormalizeLanguage(_G.Language) or "English"
local Window = Fluent:CreateWindow({
    Title = "DevSamX by Sam",
    SubTitle = "Abyss | Auto Leviathan",
    TabWidth = 160,
    Size = UDim2.fromOffset(606, 503),
    Acrylic = false,
    Theme = "Dark",
    MinimizeKey = Enum.KeyCode.G,
})
__env.AbyssWindow = Window
if __bootGui then pcall(function() __bootGui:Destroy() end) end
print("[Abyss] Interfaz creada correctamente.")
local InputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local IsMobile = InputService.TouchEnabled and not InputService.KeyboardEnabled
do
    local BASE_W, BASE_H = 606, 503
    local MARGIN = 0.95
    local function FitWindowToViewport()
        local root = Window and Window.Root
        if not (root and root:IsA("GuiObject")) then
            return
        end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            task.defer(function()
                task.wait(0.5)
                FitWindowToViewport()
            end)
            return
        end
        local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
        if not viewport or viewport.X <= 0 or viewport.Y <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local function FitAxis(base, viewportSize, fluentMin)
            local target = math.min(base, viewportSize * MARGIN)
            return math.max(target, math.min(fluentMin, viewportSize))
        end
        local width = math.floor(FitAxis(BASE_W, viewport.X, 470))
        local height = math.floor(FitAxis(BASE_H, viewport.Y, 380))
        local size = UDim2.fromOffset(width, height)
        local position = UDim2.fromOffset(
            math.floor((viewport.X - width) / 2),
            math.floor((viewport.Y - height) / 2)
        )
        root.Size = size
        root.Position = position
        Window.Size = size
        Window.Position = position
    end
    FitWindowToViewport()
    if IsMobile then
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            local lastX, lastY = camera.ViewportSize.X, camera.ViewportSize.Y
            camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
                local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
                if viewport.X == lastX and viewport.Y == lastY then
                    return
                end
                lastX, lastY = viewport.X, viewport.Y
                if Window.Maximized then
                    return
                end
                FitWindowToViewport()
            end)
        end
    end
end
local Tabs = {
    Status   = Window:AddTab({ Title = T("tab.status"), Icon = "anchor" }),
    Combat   = Window:AddTab({ Title = T("tab.combat"), Icon = "swords" }),

local function SendNotify(title, content)
    pcall(function()
        Fluent:Notify({
            Title = title,
            Content = content,
            Duration = 3,
        })
    end)
end

local function SetSectionTitle(section, key)
    local root = section and section.Container and section.Container.Parent
    local label = root and root:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
    if label then
        label.Text = T(key)
    end
end
local function SetTabTitle(tab, key)
    local label = tab and tab.Frame and tab.Frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
    if label then
        label.Text = T(key)
    end
end
